# B2-b edited documents: Word and UI observations

Recorded 944 / 944 object-model results; 44 files have separate UI observations. Initial object-model opens used DisplayAlerts=0, ReadOnly=true and Visible=false; 136 chart rows were subsequently reread with Visible=true and Document.Activate.
